# Four transformations: initial experiment results summary

**34 input programs.** Each numbered site records one transformation applied to
the original input. Transformations are evaluated separately; these rows are not
a chain of changes to the same program.

The [benchmark selection](broader-benchmark-selection.md) explains the input set;
[SOURCES.tsv](SOURCES.tsv) maps the copied inputs to their repository paths.
Every original-verification log linked below reports success.

Compare with the [latest recheck](../../latest-recheck/earlier-four-transformations/four-transformation-results-summary.md).
Each summary links to its own outputs and logs. Site numbers are local to a
program, transformation and run.

## Summary

| Transformation | ROBUST | Verification failures under review | Excluded site attempts | Inputs with no applicable site |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| [add-if-zero](#add-if-zero) | 180 | 96 | 154 | 4 |
| [add-if-one](#add-if-one) | 180 | 96 | 154 | 4 |
| [for-to-while](#for-to-while) | 1 | 6 | 15 | 23 |
| [deepen-while](#deepen-while) | 0 | 0 | 0 | 34 |
| **Total** | **361** | **198** | **323** | **65** |

The last column counts program/transformation pairs, not mutants.

`ROBUST` records successful original and transformed verification. A verification
failure remains under review until the transformation and generated annotations
have been checked: a failed proof alone does not establish a valid `NOT_ROBUST`
result. The source tables label these failures `NOT_ROBUST`; this summary does
not present them as final benchmark classifications.

Transformation, parsing, annotation-processing and verifier errors are listed
under excluded attempts. Inputs with no applicable site have their own tables.
A dash means that the run did not produce that artifact.

The [archived aggregate table](results/run-20261026-015207/initial-transformation-results.md) contains 228 recorded `NOT_ROBUST` rows.
Of these, 30 (15 for each add-if transformation) report invalid `send`/`recv`
placement. They are excluded below, leaving 198 verification failures under
review. The 358 previously excluded records comprise 293 site attempts and
65 program/transformation pairs with no site. The archived TSV files, outputs
and logs are unchanged.
